Releasing from z~0.20 while horizontally over the liner avoided the rim contact state and terminated immediately.\n**How to apply:** Identify the container semantically in agentview, use wrist to align over the interior liner/cavity, keep gripper closed during carry, and release from a high over-cavity pose around 4-6 cm above the rim rather than forcing a low descent.
